```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// tmp/g2/g2-map.mjs -- maps every G2 checklist item (contract 7.3) and the
// P2 deliverables (spec 13 row P2) to the tests that proved them, read from
// the vitest JSON reporter outputs of the five local runs P2-T3 makes:
//   node-lavapipe.json               node project, lavapipe, --coverage (the default lane's adapter)
//   node-nosubgroups-lavapipe.json   node project, lavapipe, GRAPHTY_GPU_NO_SUBGROUPS=1 over test/primitives test/layouts
//   node-nvidia.json                 node project, RTX 4070 SUPER (the GPU lane's vendor)
//   browser-swiftshader.json         browser project, Chromium on SwiftShader
//   browser-nvidia.json              browser project, Chromium on NVIDIA
// A test name is its `ancestorTitles` joined with " > " followed by its
// `title`, both read from the reporter's assertionResults; the reporter's
// own `fullName` field joins with a space and is not used.
// Prints markdown to stdout (G2.md sections 2 and 4 paste it) and exits 1
// when a run output is missing, a G2 file is absent from a run it belongs
// to, a G2 file ran zero tests, or any test in a G2 file did not pass (a
// skipped test is not evidence).
//   node tmp/g2/g2-map.mjs > tmp/g2/g2-map.md
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { relative } from "node:path";

const PKG = "/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms";
const OUT = "/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/g2";

const RUNS = [
    { key: "lavapipe", file: "node-lavapipe.json", project: "node", only: null },
    { key: "lavapipe/no-subgroups", file: "node-nosubgroups-lavapipe.json", project: "node", only: ["test/primitives/", "test/layouts/"] },
    { key: "nvidia", file: "node-nvidia.json", project: "node", only: null },
    { key: "swiftshader", file: "browser-swiftshader.json", project: "browser", only: null },
    { key: "chromium-nvidia", file: "browser-nvidia.json", project: "browser", only: null },
];

// The G2 checklist (contract 7.3, after the P2 table) item by item, then the
// P2 deliverables of spec 13 row P2 that the checklist does not name. The
// files are the contract's 5.5 assignments (P1 table for the P1 files the
// checklist re-proves at P2, P2 table for the rest).
const G2_ITEMS = [
    { id: "G2-1", item: "planUpload unit tests for every path x caps table (CAPS_TABLES, which gains CAPS_INTEL_XE at P2-T2)", files: ["test/memory/upload-plan.test.ts"] },
    { id: "G2-2", item: "the 1D / 2D boundary and the WGSL linear_id test (lavapipe in the default lane; the browser leg on SwiftShader and NVIDIA Chromium)", files: ["test/kernel/dispatch.test.ts", "test/kernel/linear-id.test.ts", "test/browser/skeleton.test.ts"] },
    { id: "G2-3", item: "reduce and thread-per-row segmentedReduce equal their oracles at scaled sizes incl. all-equal keys and one hub row, both twins in-process, bitwise across two runs", files: ["test/primitives/reduce.test.ts", "test/primitives/segmented-reduce.test.ts", "test/kernel/determinism.test.ts"] },
    { id: "G2-4", item: "the UniformBlock negative test rejected on Chromium (accepted on Dawn-node, recorded) and the state round trip green on both runtimes", files: ["test/browser/uniform-layout.test.ts", "test/kernel/uniform-layout.test.ts", "test/kernel/state-roundtrip.test.ts", "test/browser/state-roundtrip.test.ts"] },
    { id: "G2-5", item: "E_DEVICE_LOST mid-readback on both runtimes with recovery from a fresh adapter", files: ["test/device/lost.test.ts", "test/browser/lost.test.ts"] },
    { id: "G2-6", item: "a bad bind group rejects its own batch's readback under Dawn-node (thrown from the next assertReady() in the browser)", files: ["test/kernel/batch.test.ts", "test/browser/batch.test.ts"] },
    { id: "G2-7", item: "leak counter 0 after release and dispose (and every Lease buffer back in the pool)", files: ["test/leak.test.ts", "test/memory/lease.test.ts"] },
    { id: "G2-8", item: "the segmented-reduce sabotage rows fail their oracle test by >= 10x; coverage.test.ts green with P2 listed; the noise-floor rows (G2.md section 5)", files: ["test/sabotage/segmented-reduce.test.ts", "test/sabotage/coverage.test.ts", "test/noise-floor.test.ts"] },
    { id: "P2-D1", item: "deliverable: the WGSL compile matrix on both runtimes (every pipeline key of the node suite is covered by OVERRIDE_MATRIX: the test/setup/global.ts teardown throws otherwise, so a green node run is the proof)", files: ["test/kernel/wgsl-compile.test.ts", "test/browser/compile-matrix.test.ts"] },
    { id: "P2-D2", item: "deliverable: the bind-group budget (<= 8 storage entries per stage; the 3.10.1 counts exactly)", files: ["test/kernel/bind-group-budget.test.ts"] },
    { id: "P2-D3", item: "deliverable: UniformRing, Profiler, pipeline warm-up, residentBytes and its warning", files: ["test/kernel/uniform-ring.test.ts", "test/kernel/profiler.test.ts", "test/memory/residency.test.ts", "test/kernel/pipeline-cache.test.ts"] },
];

/**
 * Reads one run's JSON reporter output into a map from package-relative file path to its results.
 * @param {{ key: string, file: string }} run - the run descriptor
 * @returns {{ doc: any, files: Map<string, { status: string, seconds: number, tests: { name: string, status: string }[] }> } | null} null when the file is absent
 */
function loadRun(run) {
    const path = `${OUT}/${run.file}`;
    if (!existsSync(path)) {
        return null;
    }
    const doc = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8"));
    const files = new Map();
    for (const tr of doc.testResults) {
        const rel = relative(PKG, tr.name).split("\\").join("/");
        const tests = tr.assertionResults.map((a) => ({
            name: a.ancestorTitles.concat([a.title]).join(" > "),
            status: a.status,
        }));
        files.set(rel, { status: tr.status, seconds: (tr.endTime - tr.startTime) / 1000, tests });
    }
    return { doc, files };
}

/**
 * The vitest project a test file belongs to (contract 2.5 include lists).
 * @param {string} file - package-relative path
 * @returns {"node" | "browser"} the project
 */
function projectOf(file) {
    return file.startsWith("test/browser/") ? "browser" : "node";
}

/**
 * The runs that include a file.
 * @param {string} file - package-relative path
 * @returns {typeof RUNS} the runs
 */
function runsFor(file) {
    return RUNS.filter((r) => r.project === projectOf(file) && (r.only === null || r.only.some((p) => file.startsWith(p))));
}

let ok = true;
const loaded = new Map();
for (const run of RUNS) {
    const data = loadRun(run);
    if (data === null) {
        ok = false;
        console.log(`- MISSING run output ${OUT}/${run.file} (${run.key})`);
    }
    loaded.set(run.key, data);
}

console.log("## Run summaries");
console.log("");
console.log("| Run | Files | Tests | Passed | Failed | Pending or skipped | Wall (s) |");
console.log("| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |");
for (const run of RUNS) {
    const data = loaded.get(run.key);
    if (data === null) {
        console.log(`| ${run.key} | (missing) | | | | | |`);
        continue;
    }
    const d = data.doc;
    let first = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    let last = 0;
    for (const tr of d.testResults) {
        first = Math.min(first, tr.startTime);
        last = Math.max(last, tr.endTime);
    }
    const wall = d.testResults.length === 0 ? 0 : (last - first) / 1000;
    console.log(`| ${run.key} | ${d.testResults.length} | ${d.numTotalTests} | ${d.numPassedTests} | ${d.numFailedTests} | ${d.numPendingTests} | ${wall.toFixed(1)} |`);
}
console.log("");

const summaryPath = `${PKG}/coverage/coverage-summary.json`;
if (existsSync(summaryPath)) {
    const total = JSON.parse(readFileSync(summaryPath, "utf8")).total;
    console.log(`Coverage of the lavapipe --coverage run (coverage/coverage-summary.json; thresholds 80 / 80 / 75 / 80): lines ${total.lines.pct}%, functions ${total.functions.pct}%, branches ${total.branches.pct}%, statements ${total.statements.pct}%`);
} else {
    console.log("Coverage: coverage/coverage-summary.json not found (the --coverage run did not complete)");
    ok = false;
}
console.log("");

console.log("## Per-file durations of the lavapipe node run (slowest first)");
console.log("");
const lavapipe = loaded.get("lavapipe");
if (lavapipe !== null) {
    const rows = Array.from(lavapipe.files.entries()).sort((a, b) => b[1].seconds - a[1].seconds);
    let total = 0;
    for (const [, r] of rows) {
        total += r.seconds;
    }
    console.log("| File | Tests | Seconds |");
    console.log("| --- | --- | --- |");
    for (const [file, r] of rows.slice(0, 15)) {
        console.log(`| \`${file}\` | ${r.tests.length} | ${r.seconds.toFixed(1)} |`);
    }
    console.log("");
    console.log(`Sum of per-file times: ${total.toFixed(1)} s over ${rows.length} files (files run in parallel forks, so the wall time above is smaller).`);
}
console.log("");

console.log("## G2 items and the tests that proved them");
console.log("");
for (const item of G2_ITEMS) {
    console.log(`### ${item.id} -- ${item.item}`);
    console.log("");
    for (const file of item.files) {
        const parts = [];
        const names = [];
        const statusByName = new Map();
        for (const run of runsFor(file)) {
            const data = loaded.get(run.key);
            const r = data === null ? undefined : data.files.get(file);
            if (r === undefined) {
                ok = false;
                parts.push(`${run.key}: ABSENT`);
                continue;
            }
            const passed = r.tests.filter((t) => t.status === "passed").length;
            if (r.tests.length === 0 || passed !== r.tests.length || r.status !== "passed") {
                ok = false;
            }
            parts.push(`${run.key}: ${r.status} ${passed}/${r.tests.length} (${r.seconds.toFixed(1)} s)`);
            for (const t of r.tests) {
                if (!statusByName.has(t.name)) {
                    names.push(t.name);
                    statusByName.set(t.name, []);
                }
                statusByName.get(t.name).push(`${run.key}: ${t.status}`);
            }
        }
        console.log(`- \`${file}\` -- ${parts.join("; ")}`);
        for (const name of names) {
            const notPassed = statusByName.get(name).filter((s) => !s.endsWith(": passed"));
            const flag = notPassed.length === 0 ? "" : ` [${notPassed.join(", ")}]`;
            console.log(`    - ${name}${flag}`);
        }
    }
    console.log("");
}
console.log(ok ? "RESULT: every G2 file ran in every run it belongs to and every test passed." : "RESULT: gaps above (a missing run, an absent file, a zero-test file or a non-passed test).");
process.exit(ok ? 0 : 1);
```

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// tmp/g2/check-noise-rows.mjs -- the G2 item "its noise-floor rows recorded
// from the three adapters" for segmented-reduce: lists the per-adapter raw
// outputs test/fixtures/noise/segmented-reduce-*.json (contract 2.5 / 5.2
// shape { kernel, fixture, adapterClass, dtype, values }) and the rows of
// benchmarks/results/noise-floor.json whose kernel is "segmented-reduce"
// (contract 5.6 shape), asserts the three adapters of spec 11.5 (NVIDIA Dawn,
// lavapipe Dawn, SwiftShader Chromium) appear in the fixtures AND in the
// cross-adapter rows, checks every tolerance based on one of these rows obeys
// the 5.6 rule (value <= 10 x basis maxRelError), and prints markdown for
// G2.md section 5. Exit 0 iff every assertion holds.
//   node tmp/g2/check-noise-rows.mjs
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const PKG = "/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms";
const KERNEL = "segmented-reduce";
// adapterClass(caps) is "<vendor>-<architecture>-<runtime>" (contract 5.2):
// nvidia-<arch>-node, mesa-software-node, google-swiftshader-browser.
const REQUIRED = [
    { label: "NVIDIA Dawn (node)", test: (c) => c.startsWith("nvidia-") && c.endsWith("-node") },
    { label: "lavapipe Dawn (node)", test: (c) => c.includes("software") && c.endsWith("-node") },
    { label: "SwiftShader Chromium (browser)", test: (c) => c.includes("swiftshader") && c.endsWith("-browser") },
];

let ok = true;
const fixturesDir = join(PKG, "test/fixtures/noise");
const files = readdirSync(fixturesDir).filter((f) => f.startsWith(`${KERNEL}-`) && f.endsWith(".json")).sort();
const fixtures = files.map((f) => {
    const doc = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(fixturesDir, f), "utf8"));
    return { file: f, fixture: doc.fixture, adapterClass: doc.adapterClass, dtype: doc.dtype, count: doc.values.length };
});
console.log("Fixtures (test/fixtures/noise):");
console.log("");
console.log("| File | Fixture | Adapter class | dtype | Values |");
console.log("| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |");
for (const f of fixtures) {
    console.log(`| \`${f.file}\` | ${f.fixture} | ${f.adapterClass} | ${f.dtype} | ${f.count} |`);
}
console.log("");
const classes = fixtures.map((f) => f.adapterClass);
for (const req of REQUIRED) {
    const hit = classes.find((c) => req.test(c));
    if (hit === undefined) {
        ok = false;
    }
    console.log(`- fixture from ${req.label}: ${hit === undefined ? "MISSING" : hit}`);
}

const doc = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(PKG, "benchmarks/results/noise-floor.json"), "utf8"));
const rows = doc.rows.filter((r) => r.kernel === KERNEL);
console.log("");
console.log(`Rows of benchmarks/results/noise-floor.json with kernel "${KERNEL}" (recordedAt ${doc.recordedAt}):`);
console.log("");
console.log("| id | fixture | comparison | a | b | maxRelError | maxAbsError | samples |");
console.log("| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |");
for (const r of rows) {
    console.log(`| ${r.id} | ${r.fixture} | ${r.comparison} | ${r.a} | ${r.b} | ${r.maxRelError} | ${r.maxAbsError} | ${r.samples} |`);
}
console.log("");
const crossClasses = new Set();
for (const r of rows) {
    if (r.comparison === "cross-adapter") {
        crossClasses.add(r.a);
        crossClasses.add(r.b);
    }
}
for (const req of REQUIRED) {
    const hit = Array.from(crossClasses).find((c) => req.test(c));
    if (hit === undefined) {
        ok = false;
    }
    console.log(`- cross-adapter row covering ${req.label}: ${hit === undefined ? "MISSING" : hit}`);
}
console.log(`- twin rows: ${rows.filter((r) => r.comparison === "twin").length}`);

const tolerances = Object.entries(doc.tolerances === undefined ? {} : doc.tolerances).filter(([, t]) => rows.some((r) => r.id === t.basis));
console.log("");
console.log("Tolerances whose basis is one of these rows:");
console.log("");
for (const [id, t] of tolerances) {
    const basis = rows.find((r) => r.id === t.basis);
    const valid = t.value <= 10 * basis.maxRelError;
    if (!valid) {
        ok = false;
    }
    console.log(`- ${id}: value ${t.value}, basis ${t.basis} (maxRelError ${basis.maxRelError}), factor ${t.factor} -> ${valid ? "valid (<= 10x the basis)" : "INVALID (> 10x the basis)"}`);
}
if (tolerances.length === 0) {
    console.log("- none recorded (the segmented-reduce test derives its floor from the rows directly)");
}
console.log("");
console.log(ok ? "RESULT: the three adapters are present in the fixtures and the cross-adapter rows." : "RESULT: a required adapter or row is missing, or a tolerance exceeds 10x its basis.");
process.exit(ok ? 0 : 1);
```

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/g2/check-gpu-report.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// tmp/g2/check-gpu-report.mjs -- asserts that the gpu-report.json inside a
// downloaded gpu-results-<run id> artifact (contract 6.6 shape) is the NVIDIA
// T4 report the G2 gate needs ("GPU lane green with GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=nvidia
// and gpu-report.json uploaded") and prints the markdown for G2.md section 3.
//   gh run download <run id> -n gpu-results-<run id> -D tmp/g2/gpu-results-<run id>
//   node tmp/g2/check-gpu-report.mjs tmp/g2/gpu-results-<run id>
// Exit 0 iff every check passes; 1 when one fails or the file is absent; 2 on usage.
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

/**
 * Finds gpu-report.json anywhere under a directory (actions/upload-artifact
 * keeps the paths relative to the common ancestor of its `path` list).
 * @param {string} dir - the artifact directory
 * @returns {string | null} the path, or null
 */
function findReport(dir) {
    const stack = [dir];
    while (stack.length > 0) {
        const d = stack.pop();
        for (const name of readdirSync(d)) {
            const p = join(d, name);
            if (statSync(p).isDirectory()) {
                stack.push(p);
            } else if (name === "gpu-report.json") {
                return p;
            }
        }
    }
    return null;
}

const dir = process.argv[2];
if (dir === undefined) {
    console.error("usage: node tmp/g2/check-gpu-report.mjs <artifact directory>");
    process.exit(2);
}
const path = findReport(dir);
if (path === null) {
    console.error(`no gpu-report.json under ${dir}`);
    process.exit(1);
}
const r = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8"));
const limits = ["maxBufferSize", "maxStorageBufferBindingSize", "maxStorageBuffersPerShaderStage", "maxComputeWorkgroupsPerDimension"];
const adapter = r.adapter === undefined || r.adapter === null ? {} : r.adapter;
const policy = r.policy === undefined || r.policy === null ? {} : r.policy;
const deviceLimits = r.deviceLimits === undefined || r.deviceLimits === null ? {} : r.deviceLimits;
const smi = r.nvidiaSmi === undefined || r.nvidiaSmi === null ? {} : r.nvidiaSmi;
const checks = [
    ["ok === true", r.ok === true],
    ["policy.raw === \"nvidia\" (the lane's GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE)", policy.raw === "nvidia"],
    ["adapter.vendor === \"nvidia\"", adapter.vendor === "nvidia"],
    ["adapter.software === false", adapter.software === false],
    ["runnerClass === \"gpu-linux-t4\" (GRAPHTY_RUNNER_CLASS of gpu.yml)", r.runnerClass === "gpu-linux-t4"],
    ["deviceLimits carries the four limits", limits.every((k) => typeof deviceLimits[k] === "number")],
    ["nvidiaSmi.available === true", smi.available === true],
    ["reason === null", r.reason === null],
];
let ok = true;
console.log(`gpu-report.json at ${path}`);
console.log("");
console.log("| Check | Result |");
console.log("| --- | --- |");
for (const [label, pass] of checks) {
    ok = ok && pass;
    console.log(`| \`${label}\` | ${pass ? "pass" : "FAIL"} |`);
}
console.log("");
console.log(`- adapter: ${adapter.description} (vendor ${adapter.vendor}, architecture ${adapter.architecture}, subgroups ${adapter.subgroupMinSize}-${adapter.subgroupMaxSize})`);
console.log(`- features: ${(Array.isArray(adapter.features) ? adapter.features : []).join(", ")}`);
console.log(`- deviceLimits: ${JSON.stringify(deviceLimits)}`);
console.log(`- roundTripMs: ${r.roundTripMs}; nvidia-smi max utilisation ${smi.maxUtilization}%, max memory ${smi.maxMemoryUsedMiB} MiB over ${Array.isArray(smi.samples) ? smi.samples.length : 0} samples`);
console.log(`- webgpu ${r.webgpu}, node ${r.node}, runnerClass ${r.runnerClass}`);
process.exit(ok ? 0 : 1);
```

````markdown
# G2 -- gate record for P2 (batch + dispatch infrastructure)

Phase P2 of `design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md` (section 13, row P2) as
built against `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-14-webgpu-p0-p3-interfaces.md`
(the contract: section 7.3 rows P2-T1, P2-T2 and P2-T3, and the G2
checklist that follows them). Written by P2-T3 on {{date}}. Plain ASCII.

The rule of this record (spec 10.4): every number is measured and names
the command or the GitHub run it came from; a missed target is never
relaxed here -- it is re-fixed by an owner decision in section 6, or the
phase stays open. The same rule covers every tolerance of section 5.

## 0. Gate status

| Item | G2 checklist text (contract 7.3) | Proof | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| G2-1 | `planUpload` unit tests for every path x caps table | section 4 | pass |
| G2-2 | the 1D / 2D boundary and the WGSL `linear_id` test | section 4 | pass |
| G2-3 | `reduce` and thread-per-row `segmentedReduce` equal their oracles at scaled sizes incl. all-equal keys and one hub row, both twins in-process, bitwise across two runs | section 4 | pass |
| G2-4 | the `UniformBlock` negative test rejected on Chromium and the state round trip green | section 4 | pass |
| G2-5 | `E_DEVICE_LOST` mid-readback on both runtimes with recovery (`test/device/lost.test.ts` and `test/browser/lost.test.ts`) | section 4 | pass |
| G2-6 | a bad bind group rejects its own batch's readback under Dawn-node | section 4 | pass |
| G2-7 | leak counter 0 after release and dispose | section 4 | pass |
| G2-8 | the segmented-reduce sabotage rows fail their oracle test by >= 10x (`test/sabotage/segmented-reduce.test.ts`, `coverage.test.ts` green with "P2" listed) and its noise-floor rows recorded from the three adapters | sections 4 and 5 | pass |
| G2-9 | the GPU lane green with `gpu-report.json` uploaded (`GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=nvidia`) | sections 1 and 3 | {{gpu-verdict}} |
| G2-10 | the default lane <= 15 min (T-12; and the GPU lane <= 20 min) | section 2 | {{ci-verdict}} |

"pass" in rows G2-1 to G2-8 means `node tmp/g2/g2-map.mjs` (section 4)
and `node tmp/g2/check-noise-rows.mjs` (section 5) exited 0 on the runs

`adapter vendor=nvidia`.

## 4. Every G2 item mapped to the tests that proved it

The item text is the contract's G2 checklist (7.3) and, after it, the P2
deliverables of spec 13 row P2 the checklist does not name. The files are
the contract's 5.5 assignments. The test names are `ancestorTitles`
joined with " > " followed by `title`, both read from the vitest JSON
reporter's `assertionResults` for each file in the run named in the line
(its own `fullName` field joins with a space and is not used), extracted by
`node tmp/g2/g2-map.mjs` from the five local runs of P2-T3: `lavapipe` (node
project on Dawn lavapipe with `--coverage` -- the default lane's adapter
and command), `lavapipe/no-subgroups` (the lane's second pass over
`test/primitives test/layouts` with `GRAPHTY_GPU_NO_SUBGROUPS=1`), `nvidia`
(node project on the RTX 4070 SUPER -- the GPU lane's vendor),
`swiftshader` (browser project, Chromium on SwiftShader) and
`chromium-nvidia` (browser project, Chromium on the RTX 4070 SUPER with the
four Vulkan flags). "passed k/k" is the reporter's count; a file absent
from a run it belongs to, a file with zero tests, or a test with any other
status would have blocked this record (the script exits 1).

{{g2-map}}

## 7. Facts recorded in CLAUDE.md at G2 ("Verified Platform Facts" -> "Settled at G2")

- `uncapturederror` delivery order for an invalid command buffer:
  synchronous under Dawn-node (the event fires before `queue.submit()`
  returns; observed on lavapipe in the default lane and on the RTX 4070
  SUPER and the Tesla T4), asynchronous in Chromium (SwiftShader and
  NVIDIA Vulkan alike; the stored error is thrown from the next
  `assertReady()` after `onSubmittedWorkDone()`) -- settled by
  `test/kernel/batch.test.ts` and `test/browser/batch.test.ts` (spec 5.7).
- The misaligned hand-written uniform struct (a `vec3f` followed by an
  `f32` declared with `@size(12)`): accepted by Dawn-node
  (`uniform_buffer_standard_layout` is in its `wgslLanguageFeatures`),
  rejected by Chromium 139 ({{uniform-reject-stage}}); `UniformBlock`'s
  generated text for the same fields compiles on both -- settled by
  `test/kernel/uniform-layout.test.ts` and
  `test/browser/uniform-layout.test.ts` (spec 5.3, R-10, D20).

## 8. How to reproduce

From `packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms` on the dev box (scripts under the
gitignored `tmp/g2/` are reproduced from the P2 plan,
`docs/superpowers/plans/<date>-webgpu-p2.md`, task P2-T3):

```
bash tmp/g2/time-default-lane.sh <overhead minutes>   # the default lane locally on lavapipe (timed, JSON reporter kept)
bash tmp/g2/evidence-nvidia.sh                         # node on the RTX 4070 SUPER, browser on NVIDIA Chromium
node tmp/g2/g2-map.mjs > tmp/g2/g2-map.md              # section 4 (and the run summaries of section 2)
node tmp/g2/check-noise-rows.mjs                       # section 5
node tmp/g2/lane-durations.mjs ci.yml master <sha>     # section 2, default lane
node tmp/g2/lane-durations.mjs gpu.yml master <sha>    # section 2, GPU lane
gh run download <gpu run id> -n gpu-results-<gpu run id> -D tmp/g2/gpu-results-<gpu run id>
node tmp/g2/check-gpu-report.mjs tmp/g2/gpu-results-<gpu run id>   # section 3
```

The default-lane environment is spec 12.2's: `GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER=llvmpipe
GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=any VK_DRIVER_FILES=/usr/share/vulkan/icd.d/lvp_icd.x86_64.json
XDG_RUNTIME_DIR=/tmp` for the node steps and `GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU=swiftshader
GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=any` for the browser step; the NVIDIA legs use
`GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=hardware` with `GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER` unset and, for
Chromium, `GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU=nvidia` with `libEGL.so.1` reachable through
`GRAPHTY_EGL_LIB_DIR` / `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` (`docs/HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md`).
````

Locate the section: `grep -n 'Verified Platform Facts' /home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/CLAUDE.md`
prints its `## ` heading line (written by P0-T3, extended by P0-T4 and P1-T7). Append the block below at the END
of that section -- immediately before the next line that starts with `## ` (or at the end of the file when it
is the last section), separated by one blank line on each side. The block is self-contained (its own
`###` sub-heading and table), so the shape of the section's existing table does not matter and no existing
line changes:

```markdown
### Settled at G2 (P2-T3; the evidence is docs/decisions/G2.md)

| Fact | Dawn-node (webgpu@0.4.0) | Chromium (Playwright chromium-1181, Chromium 139) | Settled by |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| When `uncapturederror` is delivered for an invalid command buffer | SYNCHRONOUSLY: the event fires before `queue.submit()` returns, on lavapipe (default lane) and on the RTX 4070 SUPER and the Tesla T4 alike; `CommandBatch.submit()` therefore reads the context's pending-error slot right after `submit()` and rejects ITS OWN readback with `E_VALIDATION { batchId, label }` (spec 5.7) | ASYNCHRONOUSLY: the slot is still empty when `submit()` returns; the error arrives later and is thrown from the next `assertReady()` after `queue.onSubmittedWorkDone()` (SwiftShader and NVIDIA Vulkan alike), which is why `test/setup/browser.ts` drains the slot in `afterEach` | `test/kernel/batch.test.ts` (a deliberately bad bind group rejects the same batch's readback), `test/browser/batch.test.ts` (thrown from the next `assertReady()`); first seen in the review probe `docs/research/review/probes/uncaptured-order-probe.mjs` (NVIDIA only) |
| A hand-written misaligned uniform struct (a `vec3f` followed by an `f32` declared with `@size(12)`) | ACCEPTED by `createShaderModule` and pipeline creation: Dawn-node lists `uniform_buffer_standard_layout` in `wgslLanguageFeatures`, so a uniform layout bug is invisible under Node (R-10) | REJECTED: {{uniform-reject-stage}}; Chromium 139 lacks `uniform_buffer_standard_layout`. `UniformBlock`'s generated text for the same fields compiles on both runtimes, which is why every params and state struct is generated (D20) | `test/kernel/uniform-layout.test.ts` (records the acceptance), `test/browser/uniform-layout.test.ts` (the rejection) |
```
